The bsdjail reference marks FreeBSD jail lineage as the driving use case for Linux network namespaces. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8299690 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2016-08-30 Landlock LSM RFC reply: the proof-of-concept security module is explicitly benchmarked against the FreeBSD and OpenBSD sandbox designs, with the goal of letting unprivileged processes build sandboxes comparable to those operating systems rather than just filling a seccomp gap. Verbatim: "comparable to the Seatbelt/XNU Sandbox or the OpenBSD Pledge". FreeBSD and OpenBSD sandbox models as the design target. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8299701 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2009-04-15 multicast duplication patch dispute: Neil Horman defends the existing Linux behaviour by citing cross-OS precedent, explicitly naming BSD as an operating system that has always handled multicast subscriptions this way. Verbatim: "worked in bsd, linux, and presumably various other unix and non-unix operating systems". BSD as a normative cross-platform reference for Linux socket semantics. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8299889 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2006-11-29 madwifi and ar5k thread: Nick Kossifidis asks why not work on dadwifi and adds that improving ar5k with newer madwifi versions and bug fixes will also help BSD people, connecting the two driver lineages. David Kimdon replies that dadwifi would integrate well with d80211 and calls the legacy code historical artifacts. Verbatim: "that 'll also help bsd ppl". BSD 802.11 driver lineage. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8300149 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2008-02-28 SO_REUSEADDR thread: Willy Tarreau argues that SO_REUSEPORT is a real requirement and points at the FreeBSD implementation as the model to copy, noting his patch only has to set a second bit of sk->reuse. Verbatim: "I've been encountering the need for SO_REUSEPORT as implemented on some BSD for instance". FreeBSD semantics as the design target for a new Linux socket option. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8300493 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2010-08-26 tcp select-writefds hang fix: after KOSAKI Motohiro and Ben Hutchings make the case, David Miller verifies the behaviour against BSD 4.4-Lite before applying the patch. Verbatim: "this matches what even BSD4.4-Lite does (I checked yesterday before my initial reply), so I will apply this patch". BSD release used as the acceptance criterion for a Linux TCP fix. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8300539 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2015-10-23 accept and shutdown socket thread: David A. Holland, a NetBSD developer, writes in to correct the Solaris comparison and grounds the correct behaviour in the BSD file abstraction, noting that system call logic operates on the open-file object rather than the descriptor number. Verbatim: "System call processing for operations on files involves translating a file descriptor (a number) into an open-file object". BSD struct file architecture predating POSIX. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8301087 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2011-03-16 xen network backend driver submission: Ian Campbell describes netback as the host side counterpart of the frontend driver in xen-netfront, and notes that the paravirtual protocol is shared, with frontend drivers implemented by other operating systems too. Verbatim: "The PV protocol is also implemented by frontend drivers in other OSes too, such as the BSDs and even Windows.". BSD operating systems as co-implementers of the Xen PV net protocol. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8301607 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2010-11-15 future of ethtool thread: Jeff Garzik outlines two unresolved usability problems and proposes a more flexible nictool using the same ioctls, and Ben Hutchings checks how FreeBSD handles the same configuration tasks before weighing in on API bindings. Verbatim: "I've recently been looking at FreeBSD where the sort of configuration we do through ethtool is invoked through ifconfig". FreeBSD ifconfig model as an external design reference. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8302245 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2006-04-17 softmac TX rate control patch: Daniel Drake removes the mcast_fallback field and cites Sam Leffler, the seminal BSD 802.11 architect, as the authority on why the field is meaningless, because multicast frames are never acknowledged and therefore never retransmitted. Verbatim: "Sam Leffler pointed out that this field is meaningless, because no driver will ever be retransmitting mcast frames". BSD wireless authority steering Linux ieee80211softmac. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8302247 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2010-10-28 iproute receive buffer thread: Patrick McHardy defends his 1MB default and manual override by quoting the existing Linux socket code, which deliberately mirrors BSD behaviour so that a too-large SO_RCVBUF request is clamped rather than rejected, with the inline comment explicitly invoking BSD as the reason not to error out. Verbatim: "RCVBUF/SNDBUF are treated in BSD as hints". BSD socket option semantics embedded in Linux source. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8302284 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2012-02-23 SIGSYS seccomp thread: Roland McGrath, the glibc maintainer, objects to adding an uncatchable signal and grounds the position in the long-standing BSD invariant that since 4.2BSD, the release that introduced the modern blocking concept, only SIGKILL and SIGSTOP have been uncatchable. Verbatim: "That has been just SIGKILL and SIGSTOP since 4.2BSD, which first introduced the modern concept of blocking signals". 4.2BSD signal model as a Linux design constraint. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8302308 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2010-08-15 GTSM and TCP accept thread: Stephen Hemminger works through the ways to handle stuck handshakes from a rogue peer and rejects pushing a per-peer MINTTL table down into iptables, because the affected application has to run on BSD as well and changing iptables rules would break existing firewalls. Verbatim: "a general application that needs to run on BSD, and would mess up existing firewall rules". BSD portability as a design constraint. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8303117 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2014-08-27 skb_warn_bad_offload thread: Brian Rak reports that qemu guests running FreeBSD intermittently flood the host with skb_warn_bad_offload warnings at net/core/dev.c, while Linux guests on the same hosts do not, so the trigger is tied to the guest operating system and its checksum and segmentation behaviour. Verbatim: "sometimes FreeBSD guests cause this warning to flood the host "WARNING: CPU: 5 PID: 3705 at net/core/dev.c:2238 skb_warn_bad_offload+0xc3/0xd0()". FreeBSD guest offload interaction. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8303404 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2008-12-17 urg and gso thread: Alexey Kuznetsov opens by noting that netbsd and openbsd must have some other quirk to work around the urgent-pointer 0xFFFF case, since Linux cannot set the value directly, and proposes sending the fake urgent pointer only when the range is beyond snd_nxt. Verbatim: "netbsd/openbsd have some another quirk to work this around". BSD TCP urgent data behaviour as the comparison point. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8303652 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2006-11-03 kevent design thread: Pavel Machek complains that David Miller's response amounts to keeping an unsigned long in the BSD version and rewriting it from scratch, which he calls a bad idea and suggests fixing just the disliked bit, and Miller disagrees, arguing that since the structures must be incompatible anyway the opportunity is to design something superior. Verbatim: "since we have to be structure incompatible anyways, let's design something superior if we can". BSD kevent API explicitly compared and deliberately not followed. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8303934 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2008-06-10 IP_MULTICAST_LOOP patch thread: YOSHIFUJI Hideaki rejects adding an EINVAL check on the option value by pointing at FreeBSD, which does not validate the value either, as the interoperation precedent, so Linux keeping permissive behaviour stays compatible with BSD. Verbatim: "For example, FreeBSD does not check this.". FreeBSD behaviour as the compatibility reference. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8303968 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2017-01-31 af_packet direct DMA thread: Jesper Dangaard Brouer argues that bulking is essential for performance and uses FreeBSD's netmap as the benchmark, noting that netmap also depends on transferring control between kernel and userspace and reports needing at least a bulking size of 8. Verbatim: "report that they need at least bulking size 8, to amortize the overhead". FreeBSD netmap as the design benchmark. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8304170 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2010-03-17 RPS receive packet steering thread: Changli Gao notes that with RPS Linux can now dispatch packets the way FreeBSD does via netisr, and asks whether weighted distribution is feasible; Eric Dumazet walks through the trade-offs before sketching a weighted rps_map sysfs file. Verbatim: "Now linux also can dispatch packets as FreeBSD does via netisr". FreeBSD netisr as the reference model. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8304240 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2008-06-15 r8169 descriptor thread: Denys Fedoryshchenko quotes the NetBSD source directly from cvsweb.netbsd.org, which documents that RX and TX DMA rings can hold up to 1024 descriptors, and contrasts that with Linux hardcoding 256 RX and 64 TX descriptors in r8169. Verbatim: "From NetBSD sources http://cvsweb.netbsd.org/bsdweb.cgi/~checkout~/src/sys/dev/ic/rtl8169.c?rev=1.14.2.7". NetBSD implementation as the reference for Linux r8169. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8304849 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2004-10-04 neighbour cache backport thread: David S. Miller explains why a bound-less neighbour cache is acceptable in BSD, and Harald Welte accepts that reasoning and drops his prefix-based sizing idea. Verbatim: "BSD's scheme works because when routing entries die so do the neighbour entries they point to, so they have no need for garbage collection like we do". BSD neighbour cache architecture as the comparison point. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8304990 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2004-07-15 CARP failover introduction: Evgeniy Polyakov presents a CARP implementation based on OpenBSD's CARP protocol and states upfront that it is not compatible with OpenBSD, because the upstream implementation lacks protection against repeated message sending, which his design adds with a 64 bit counter and SHA1 HMAC digest. Verbatim: "It is based on OpenBSD's CARP protocol but is not compatible with it". OpenBSD protocol lineage in Linux. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8305757 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2012-08-10 AF_UNIX sockets from a specified root thread: Alan Cox warns that the behaviour being proposed is explicitly a Linux thing and is not generally true of the AF_UNIX socket domain, and that even the permissions aspect is not guaranteed to be supported on some BSD environments, so H. Peter Anvin agrees to scope the proposal to Linux. Verbatim: "the permissions aspect isn't guaranteed to be supported on some BSD environments". BSD AF_UNIX semantics as a divergence boundary. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8305945 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2002-07-16 thread carrying Vince Laviano's IGMPv3 for Linux 2.4 announcement: he ports Wilbert de Graaf's FreeBSD IGMPv3 implementation, publishes the patch, documentation and test programs, and reports that the original FreeBSD implementation's web page is mid-migration. Verbatim: "IGMPv3 implementation for FreeBSD to Linux 2.4.x". FreeBSD IGMPv3 code ported to Linux. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8306306 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2013-02-27 low latency Ethernet device polling RFC thread: Stephen Hemminger asks whether Tom Herbert has looked at netmap, calling it a cleaner user API for the same goal, and Herbert replies that it is orthogonal since his intent is socket-based spin polling without an API change. Verbatim: "Have you looked at netmap? Seems like a cleaner user API for this.". FreeBSD netmap as the external API reference. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8306779 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Intel X533 网卡连接问题在 netdev 与 Intel-wired-lan 之间流转,报告者指出 VyOS 1.4 与 Debian 12 均无法建立链路,而 it does in OPNsense which is based on FreeBSD 13.2,并补充 no link unless I'm using FreeBSD。整串讨论以 FreeBSD 派生发行版作为可复现参照,属于 Linux 与 BSD 驱动行为对比,BSD 谱系在此作为跨系统行为证据被直接引用。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9062415 -->`
- 【Linux社区】libbpf 为 xdp_mb 类程序选择 SEC 后缀,评审中 reviewer 提出 I like the "mbuf" suggestion, even-though it conflicts with (Free)BSD mbufs,并以 mbuf has too much bsd taste 表达命名顾虑。BSD 的 mbuf 对象模型与 Linux SKB 的对应关系在此被明确讨论,属于 BSD 命名谱系对 Linux 内核代码命名决策的直接介入。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9062719 -->`
- 【Linux社区】bpftool 的 Makefile、文档构建与 .gitignore 被修正 SPDX 标识符,评审者指出 bpftool 采用 GPL 与 BSD 双许可,原文为 Bpftool is dual-licensed under GPLv2 and BSD-2-Clause,先前统一双许可的提交漏掉了这些非源码文件。该讨论记录 Linux 用户态工具链里 BSD-2-Clause 作为保留许可选项的存在,以及 SPDX 许可证清理治理中的实际执行情况。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9062850 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Homa 网络协议是否上游化的讨论里,评审者追问用户态接口与 BSD sockets 的兼容立场,原文为 You say it is not BSD socket compatible,并反问是否存在其他既有内核 API 可承担该接口角色。BSD sockets 抽象层在此作为跨内核态与用户态的接口兼容性门槛被提出,属于 BSD 接口谱系在协议接纳标准中的直接参与。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9065152 -->`
- 【Linux社区】讨论在 VLAN 接口上启用 HTB 硬件卸载时,报告者粘贴 Mellanox ConnectX-6 驱动的 modinfo 输出,其中许可字段明确写为 license: Dual BSD/GPL。该字段是 mlx5_core 驱动的 BSD 与 GPL 双许可状态在 netdev 讨论中的直接记录,虽为引用性出现,但确证了 BSD 许可在高性能网络设备驱动中的实际部署。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9065440 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Intel X533 链路故障的续帖,报告者确认 VyOS 1.4 与 Debian 12 均无法建链,却 does in OPNsense which is based on FreeBSD 13.2,并称两侧均有光信号,只是未使用 FreeBSD 时无链路。维护者建议尝试 allow_unsupported_sfp=1 模块参数,该参数为 Linux 侧 SFP 模块兼容性开关,构成 FreeBSD 驱动行为与 Linux 侧对策之间的跨系统对照。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9066455 -->`
- 【Linux社区】关于在内核中引入 Rust socket 抽象的 RFC 评审,评审者指出用户态侧必然依赖 BSD sockets 接口,原文为 I assume it is using BSD sockets to communicate with the kernel and the protocol,作者另引 USENIX ATC 论文说明原型为内部 LKM。BSD sockets 被设定为内核协议与用户态通信的默认 API 假设,属于 BSD 接口谱系在内核架构讨论中的直接引用。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9067062 -->`
- 【Linux社区】为 TI PHY 的 DT 绑定头文件追加 MIT 许可并保留 GPL-2.0-only 的补丁引发许可讨论,评审者反问为何不采用 BSD-2-Clause,原文为 What's wrong with BSD-2-Clause, the standard dual license for,维护者则指出若两个生态都需要宽松许可则应选 BSD-2-Clause,另称该许可利于 Zephyr 与 FreeBSD 等操作系统生态复用。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9067256 -->`
- 【Linux社区】评审 TCP 连接因 tw hashdance 竞争而误重置的补丁时,作者直接以 FreeBSD 实现作为参照,原文为 I took a look at FreeBSD, it uses hash table lock and per sock level lock,并指出 FreeBSD 同样需要额外同步措施。该讨论把 BSD 的 TCP 同步与锁设计引入 Linux 补丁评审作为正确性论据,属于 BSD 谱系在 Linux 网络代码评审中的直接技术引用。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9073444 -->`
- 【Linux社区】修复 dpll 子系统 netlink 转储可能的死锁时,补丁同时删除 dpll_nl.c 中冗余的 SPDX 头副本,文件 SPDX 头原文为 ((GPL-2.0 WITH Linux-syscall-note) OR BSD-3-Clause)。该声明保留 BSD-3-Clause 作为该 netlink 实现文件的有效许可选项,是 BSD 许可在 Linux 网络代码中通过 SPDX 双许可机制被正式登记的实例。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.9074006 -->`
- 【Linux社区】通用 802.11 协议栈入主线的许可证争论中,BSD 代码能否并入被正面讨论:Sam Leffler 追问对方是否使用了别处代码,David Miller 坚持改许可须获全部作者同意,称若要把他的代码用于纯 GPL 之外的用途必须经其许可,并直言 "if he wants to add BSD code to it he cannot do so",即 BSD 代码进入 Linux 无线栈前必须先解清原始作者的授权前提。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8345887 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linux 是否窃取代码的转帖中,BSD 派生源码的许可身份被点明:讨论者指出多数文件是 BSD 授权,因为是从 OpenBSD 分叉而来,原话为 "most of the files are/were BSD-licensed, because they were forked from OpenBSD",发帖人随后承认自己一直误以为 Jiri 的原补丁除了改许可文本外还含真实代码,实际只是许可文本变更,属 BSD 血统与许可证变更的辨析记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8345966 -->`
- 【Linux社区】TCP 度量指标可选化补丁评审中,BSD 套接口血统被明确定性:Eric Dumazet 反对为省内存裁掉内核套接口基础设施,原话为 "the kernel to handle arbitrary number of sockets using the venerable and slow BSD socket api",把 Linux 内核必须承载任意规模连接的底座指认为源自 BSD 的经典套接口 API,属 BSD 谱系在内核网络基础设施层面的直接陈述。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8346062 -->`
- 【Linux社区】ar5k 许可证评估公告中,BSD 系源码的原始作者身份被司法化确认:SFLC 说明已向 OpenBSD 团队就 ar5k 开发史独立问询,并称回应提供了合理基础,使其相信参与 ar5k 的 OpenBSD 开发者没有挪用代码,该实现是 OpenBSD 的原始版权作品,原话为 "OpenBSD's original copyrighted work",Michael Buesch 随即追问既然可以用这些 OpenBSD 源码是否该移植进自己的驱动框架。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8347846 -->`
- 【Linux社区】shutdown 与 close 对 accept 队列中 socket 语义的缺陷排查里,Al Viro 追溯匿名套接字数据区字段的史源:他询问是否还有人用旧的 msg_accrights 变体,并断言匿名数据传递机制自 4.3-Reno 以来就一直在,原话为 "It's been there since at least 4.3-Reno",同时指出 Solaris 早年也用 msg_accrights 传描述符而现行版本已支持 SCM_RIGHTS,是 BSD 4.3 时代套接口机制在 Linux 中的直系留痕。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8348045 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Unix 套接字传文件描述符的记账补丁讨论中,Alan Cox 指出该缺陷远早于 Linux:他说明这类 bug 在 Linux 之前早已有人在 BSD unixes 上贴出演示,原话为 "There were demonstrations of this bug posted for BSD unixes before Linux even existed",并称它与跑空 socket 缓冲区的同类问题都比 Linux 更古老,属 BSD 套接口缺陷谱系被作为 Linux 缺陷先例的正面记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8348500 -->`
- 【Linux社区】虚拟 CAN 网设备驱动入树时的许可证措辞之争中,作者坚持沿用双许可,原话为 "Dual BSD/GPL as many other code in the Linux Kernel",并称内核本身处于 GPLv2、他们只是参照 Linus 的心意引用顶层 COPYING 文件;评审方追问是否需允许只选 GPLv2,作者答称 Dual BSD/GPL 比纯 GPL 给程序员更多权利,且在内核上下文中与 GPL 代码行为一致,属 BSD 双许可在 CAN 子系统的现场留痕。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8349715 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Landlock LSM 非特权沙箱提案的评审中,BSD 系沙箱被直接抬为对标对象:提案说明要把任何进程包括非特权进程创建沙箱的能力定位到与 Apple Seatbelt 及 XNU Sandbox、OpenBSD Pledge 相当的水平,原话为 "comparable to the Seatbelt/XNU Sandbox or the OpenBSD Pledge",Andy Lutomirski 随即要求给出 eBPF API 的具体描述,是 BSD 家族沙箱接口成为 Linux 新 LSM 设计基准的记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8350547 -->`
- 【Linux社区】ip addr 前缀解析报错的讨论中,Berkeley 与 BSD 血统被明确点出:Ben Hutchings 说明单点号缺失地址的处理是 Berkeley 扩展、经由 BSD 传播并由 inet_aton 实现,原话为 "It's a Berkeley extension which spread via BSD and its inet_aton()",并认为该行为未被任何 RFC 规定,Krzysztof Halasa 据此主张多年来一直如此、不应改动,Stephen Hemminger 最终保留带更多错误检查的原实现。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8350652 -->`
- 【Linux社区】TCP 紧急数据与 GSO 交互补丁的评审中,BSD 行为被明确抬为设计判据:Alexey Kuznetsov 指出当前做法可能让紧急模式完全失效,而 BSD 行为只会打破本已错误的应用,原话为 "While the BSD behaviour would only break broken applications",Kuznetsov 进一步称 NetBSD 与 OpenBSD 大概另有变通怪癖,最终认定这是只能以痛苦方式解决的致命冲突,并把 SO_OOBINLINE 设为默认的建议摆上桌。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8350735 -->`
- 【Linux社区】ath5k 转为仅 GPLv2 的公告引发许可证反驳,Valdis Kletnieks 指出反向工程大多由 OpenBSD 团队完成,并称自己不清楚 Jiri 是否有权移除 BSD 及其他许可,原话为 "it's unclear to me if Jiri is even allowed to remove the BSD/other license",同时认为 Jiri 可只把自己的代码以 GPLv2 发布,但整个文件集合理应仍为 dual BSD/GPLv2,属 BSD 系代码被单方收窄许可的治理争议实录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8350855 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mellanox mthca HCA 底层驱动进入内核树时,文件头明确给出二选一许可:软件可在 GNU GPL 第二版或 OpenIB.org BSD 许可之间择一,原话为 "or the OpenIB.org BSD license below",并附完整 BSD 再分发与免责条款,版权声明列 Topspin Communications 2004 年保留所有权利,是 InfiniBand 生态以 BSD 许可文本随厂商驱动进入主线网络子系统的实例。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8351007 -->`
- 【Linux社区】i40evf 驱动上游化时,David Miller 转述了双许可驱动的通行模式,原话为 "Some Linux drivers are dual-licenced under a permissive licence and GPL",并说明所有对该类代码的贡献大概也须同时双许可,这样厂商就能在其他操作系统与专有许可下继续复用核心硬件代码;发件人同时质疑为何内核会接受版权转让条件,构成 BSD 风格宽松许可与版权归一化在内核网设备驱动治理上的直接碰撞。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8351124 -->`
- 【Linux社区】ARP 宣告与 proxy_arp 的 RFC 1027 符合性争论中,Eric Biederman 援引 RFC 原文说明该规范直接以 4.3BSD 行为为依据,引语为 "In 4.3BSD (and probably in other operating systems), a default route is possible",并指出默认路由在检查路由目标主机时不得被使用,从而论证 Linux 的 proxy_arp 行为同样不符合该 RFC,属 BSD 谱系经 IETF 标准文档传导为 Linux 行为判据的记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8351752 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 网络随机化移植补丁的评审中,许可证兼容性被明确讨论,提问者称三条款 BSD 许可至少与 GPL 兼容,Ingo Molnar 回复同意,原话为 "At least the three clause BSD license is GPL compatible",作者随后表态会尽量改用 Arjan 的函数而非 obsd 的实现;线程还转贴安全分析对移植质量的质疑,称 "I'm not sure how the OpenBSD code is better in any way",点出它与 Linux 用同一个 half_md4_transform。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8351840 -->`
- 【Linux社区】acx100 是否纳入主线与通用 802.11 栈选型的讨论中,BSD 派生血统被明确点出:Jeff Garzik 在追问当前可用 802.11 栈时列出,原话为 "Atheros has one, said to be derived from BSD, is there any others",并把决策指向 wireless-2.6 中的 HostAP 与 DaveM 的 802.11 栈模板,属厂商无线栈的 BSD 谱系被纳入主线选型考量的记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8351979 -->`
- 【Linux社区】NETLINK 套接口不遵守 SO_RCVLOWAT 的追问里,David Miller 把 BSD 行为定为最终判据,原话为 "And when all else fails BSD's behavior is what we use to determine",并解释多个套接字选项只是存在 socket 里的状态位,为每个选项写协议特定检查与回调只会是无用膨胀,同时说完全可以查 BSD、它对若干套接字选项的行为与 Linux 完全一致,属 BSD socket 语义作为 Linux 设计基准的正面记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8352398 -->`
- 【Linux社区】XDP 之争的转发讨论中,BSD 系旁路方案被引作对照:被引用的意见指出 netmap 一类旁路方案同样能把包重新注回内核栈,原话为 "Bypass solutions like netmap can also inject packets back to kernel stack again",而质疑者随即表达根本性顾虑,称一旦包离开内核所有安全保证就失效,属 BSD 网络旁路框架持续充当 XDP 安全模型讨论参照系的记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8352560 -->`
- 【Linux社区】McHardy 在 ppp SC_MUST_COMPRESS 补丁里把 if_ppp.h 头文件注释整体替换为 CMU 来源的 BSD 许可证文本,引发对其 GPL 兼容性的追问。引语: "Why do you change the license, especially to something GPL-incompatible?" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8505983 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Stephen Hemminger 在 SYN-ACK 抑制讨论中援引 BSD socket 既有模型,认为按地址限制连接应对应 BSD 上的某个 socket option,并建议直接套用该模型。引语: "Some version of BSD sockets had this behaviour, perhaps you should use the same model." `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8506486 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Eric Biederman 评审 SCM_AUDIT 等 socket 控制消息时反问,为何不直接用 BSD 既有的 SCM_CREDS 通道传递对端凭据与元数据。引语: "is there a reason you do not add a patch to pass the bsd SCM_CREDS?" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8506518 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David S. Miller 就 2.6.8 TCP/IP 异常定性,指真正问题出在 OpenBSD 而非 Linux,且该缺陷早有记录。引语: "It was found to in fact be OpenBSD, this bug has been well known for some time too." `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8506608 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Vlad Yasevich 在 sctp 源地址选择讨论中转述论据,指出弱主机模型在链路上与 BSD 对端共存时被实际观测到,并据此否弃源地址多归属提议。引语: "even with a BSD peer" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8506865 -->`
- 【Linux社区】John Sage 就 SCO 与 IBM 争讼中关于 BPF 血统的断言向 netdev 求证,焦点正是 Linux BPF 是否承自 BSD 许可证体系及其与 System V 的关系。引语: "licensed under the BSD license terms that never was part of SysV at any time" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8507387 -->`
- 【Linux社区】r8169 分支性能讨论中,报告者称 Linux 仍落后于 FreeBSD,Francois Romieu 请其用 strace -T 抓取 iperf 过程并与 FreeBSD 版本逐项对照。引语: "compare it against the FreeBSD one" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8507561 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Stephen Hemminger 在 BPF syscall 系列评审中建议把 man 页改成 FreeBSD 的 bpf 手册页格式,因为那样更易读也便于审阅。引语: "similar to FreeBSD man page: http://www.freebsd.org/cgi/man.cgi?bpf(4)" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8507733 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Joerg Roedel 论证 EtherIP 隧道用例时,引用 David Miller 指出 OpenBSD 互操作问题真实存在,并补述 NetBSD 实现同样不检查入向头部。引语: "The interoperability issue with the BSDs is real" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8507878 -->`
- 【Linux社区】IPv6 默认路由超时排查中,John Dykstra 转述调试结论,定位到路由侧的 OpenBSD 无线驱动误以为自己已广播路由通告。引语: "the router's (openbsd) wireless driver -- it thinks it is broadcasting router advertisements" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8507882 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David S. Miller 就无自有 IPv6 地址的隧道设计征询意见时,描述 FreeBSD 复用首个物理接口链路本地地址并以 scope identifier 消歧的做法。引语: "they take the link-local address of the first physical interface" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8508018 -->`
- 【Linux社区】linux@horizon.com 以 FreeBSD 的 SO_NOSIGPIPE 为起点发起 SIGPIPE 与 MSG_NOSIGNAL 清理提案,主张它比全局关闭 SIGPIPE 更适合库使用。引语: "FreeBSD has a useful SOL_SOCKET option, SO_NOSIGPIPE" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8508411 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Kazunori Miyazawa 发布 USAGI 3.1,该 Linux IPv6 栈的谱系直接系于 BSD 世系的 KAME 项目与 WIDE Project。引语: "We are tightly collaborating with WIDE Project[2], KAME Project[3]" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8508829 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Petr Tesarik 反驳 urg 指针语义实现时指出,Linux 与 BSD 的读法一致,都把该偏移当作指向带外数据之后首字节的指针,而非流内绝对偏移。引语: "the offset as a pointer to the first byte after the" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8509178 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Roland Dreier 在同一路由通告丢包排查中权衡两端,认为重启发送侧的 OpenBSD 路由器即可恢复,故更倾向归咎发送端。引语: "given that bouncing the driver on the sender (openbsd router) fixes things" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8509248 -->`
- 【Linux社区】IPv6 扩展头权限讨论中,转述 socket API 的历史出处质疑其规范性,称源自 BSD 的经典 socket 接口本身从未经过 IETF 或 IANA 立项。引语: "the classic socket API's that come down from BSD are not ietf'ed or iana'ed" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8509596 -->`
- 【Linux社区】jamal 在 qdisc 统计兼容方案讨论中认可按 RTA_PAYLOAD 判断旧结构的做法,并援引 BSD 的做法说明兼容性维护方式。引语: "Some of the BSDs do this to maintain old compat" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8509628 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Arjan van de Ven 指出 InfiniBand 双许可证文本需澄清 BSD 分支仅在完全脱离 Linux 内核时适用,Bryan O'Sullivan 提议用 EXPORT_SYMBOL_GPL 标注 IB 导出符号落实该区分。引语: "only applies when used entirely outside the linux kernel" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8509775 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Laight 讨论 sockaddr_storage 实际实例化风险时援引 NetBSD 的立场,认为该类型本不应被真正构造。引语: "the NetBSD view was that 'sockaddr_storage' should never actually be instantiated" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8510692 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Seth David Schoen 提议让子网最低地址回归普通单播,论证时引 RFC 1112 关于 BSD 世系使用非标准广播形式的历史记述。引语: "All operating systems since 4.3BSD, including all the current BSD OSes, now use the standard IP broadcast address" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8511130 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Michal Kubecek 核对 netdevice.h 内核文档时转述其注释,指出接口标志位被标注为沿用 BSD 风格,并质疑私有标志与 if.h 的对应说明错位。引语: "Interface flags (a la BSD)" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8511349 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Ben Hutchings 在 IPv6 TCLASS 套接字选项一致性争议中,以 FreeBSD、Darwin 与 Windows 三家实现作交叉验证,论证四字节载荷写法已成事实标准。引语: "FreeBSD/Darwin and Windows also put 4 bytes of data in a IPV6_TCLASS cmsg" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8512743 -->`
- 【Linux社区】QRTR 命名服务迁入内核引发许可证处理,Manivannan Sadhasivam 说明原用户态代码以 BSD3 发布,迁入 Linux 内核时改采 BSD 与 GPL 双许可证。引语: "For migrating it to Linux kernel, I have adapted Dual BSD/GPL license" `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8513642 -->`
- 【Linux社区】ath5k pcu kerneldoc cleanup from Lee Jones lands in ath-next via Kalle Valo, and the Cc roster still credits an OpenBSD committer as steward of the ath5k lineage inherited from madwifi. Quote: Cc: Reyk Floeter <reyk@openbsd.org> `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8579335 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jakub Kicinski proposes an XDP software offload model with a new stack driver; Alexei Starovoitov endorses the direction and explicitly permits a dual GPL plus BSD licence for the added driver, a BSD permissive licensing path inside netdev. Quote: Patch 4 - if you want to do dual gpl+bsd `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8579760 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Greg KH asks Luis Chamberlain to drop the GPL boilerplate from a new selftests sysfs module; Chamberlain points to license-rules.rst which allows individual files to be dual licensed under a compatible GPL variant plus a permissive licence such as BSD, and defends his copyleft-next and GPLv2 dual strategy vetted by SUSE and Red Hat attorneys. Quote: a permissive license like BSD, MIT etc. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8581777 -->`
- 【Linux社区】In a PHY reset and device-tree compatible debate, Florian Fainelli rejects the claim that Linux has a bootloader-independent requirement, arguing that support for an ethernet-phy identifier compatible string is not Linux specific and other operating systems such as FreeBSD could parse it the same way. Quote: so could FreeBSD or any OS for that matter `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8582476 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Thomas Higdon adds a TCP_INFO counter for out-of-order packets received, and David Miller applies it, explicitly noting that the counter mirrors the same statistic already exposed by NetBSD TCP_INFO under the same name, a direct NetBSD to Linux API crossport. Quote: this is similar to the counter in NetBSD TCP_INFO `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8582851 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Alexander Lobakin sends a six-patch licence cleanup for the QLogic qed and qede ethernet drivers, correcting wrong SPDX tags and stripping GPL boilerplates to state the true dual licence of the tree, which matters because these drivers are one of the few large networking codebases carrying a BSD three clause alternative to GPLv2. Quote: source code is dual licensed under GPL-2.0/BSD-3-Clause `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8583302 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Alexei Starovoitov bpf-next pull request to David Miller includes the relicensing of libbpf under LGPL-2.1 or BSD-2-Clause so that Apache 2 projects can depend on it, a deliberate BSD permissive clause added to a widely embedded Linux user space library. Quote: relicense libbpf as LGPL-2.1 OR BSD-2-Clause `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8585107 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Johannes Lundberg proposes EPOLLEMPTY for TCP write buffer drain latency, citing that FreeBSD already ships the equivalent kqueue EVFILT_EMPTY filter; Eric Dumazet rejects the extra indirect call, says Linux timestamping already covers it, and counters that timestamping should be ported to FreeBSD instead. Quote: Our timestamping infrastructure should be ported to FreeBSD instead `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8587565 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Manivannan Sadhasivam migrates the Qualcomm IPC Router nameservice from userspace into net/qrtr so ath11k Wi-Fi works without a daemon, and states the licensing bridge he used to move BSD-3 code into the kernel: the upstream userspace ns.c is BSD-3 licensed and he adapted a dual BSD or GPL notice for the in-kernel port. Quote: The original userspace code is published under BSD3 license `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8588361 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Boris Pismenny defends copyless TLS device sendpage, arguing FreeBSD makes the same behaviour the default and that the performance gain justifies it; David Miller refuses to weaken TLS signature correctness for Linux and declines to trade feature policy against FreeBSD behaviour, ending the BSD comparison as irrelevant to netdev. Quote: If people want corrupt TLS signatures in legitimate use cases, and FreeBSD allows it, so be it. `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8588502 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Serge Semin adds rtl8211e RX and TX delay configuration via hidden MDIO extension page registers, documenting the bits from the PHY datasheet and cross checking them against a FreeBSD review thread on the same register, so a FreeBSD mailing list patch becomes upstream evidence for Linux PHY register layout. Quote: There is also a freebsd-folks discussion regarding this register `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8591179 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Josh Triplett cuts ena admin command polling from 5 ms to 10 us, dropping adapter init on a c5.12xlarge from 173.6 ms to 1.9 ms; Zorik Machulsky asks for a gentler backoff, and Triplett asks that the same fix be ported to the EFA driver and to the FreeBSD port, extending a Linux performance fix into BSD trees. Quote: you might wish to make a similar change to the EFA driver, and to the FreeBSD drivers `<!-- ROW_MINE: netdev.messages.8593008 -->`
